Per match: list of all markets with their outcomes + winner highlight (from `market_outcomes.result`), an inline 24h SVG chart, and a per-market **Odds history** button (30-day window since 2026-07-02; was 7-day) → `/admin/logs/matches/[id]/markets/[marketId]` with the full 7-day chronological table. **Feed log** button opens `/admin/logs/matches/[id]/feed` with a kind-filterable `<details>` viewer of raw AMQP XML. Counts and feed-message lookups join on `event_urn = matches.provider_urn` so rows inserted before the auto-mapper created the match (race condition) are still surfaced. feed-ingester writes every match-scoped kind to `feed_messages`; hourly `runFeedMessageCleanup` goroutine sweeps + backfills `match_id`. Uniform 7-day retention since `received_at`. |

| Admin: FE Settings (markets display order + market groups) | Live; real per-sport tab set since 2026-09-05 | `/admin/fe-settings/markets-order` per-sport, per-tab editor with HTML5 drag-and-drop. **Each sport is offered the tabs its own offer produces, not a fixed list.** Until 2026-09-05 the screen enumerated `match` + `map_1..map_5` + `top` for every sport — the esports shape — so football was offered five Map tabs no football fixture has ever had, while the tabs bettors were actually looking at (1st half, 2nd half, Corners, Yellow cards, Players — derived from Fonbet's `variant` sub-events since that line landed) could be neither ordered nor reordered. Scopes now: `match` (the base event), `map_<N>` (one independent list per map tab), `fb_<kinds>` (one per Fonbet sub-event, including the collapsed `fb_players`; migration 0106), `top` (curated highlights, empty by default), `custom_<key>` (admin-created curated groups). **Discovery, not enumeration**: the tab set is not stored anywhere — the match page derives it per request from the markets it is about to render — so [`services/api/src/lib/fe-market-scopes.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/fe-market-scopes.ts) re-derives it over the sport's CURRENT offer using the same shared module the storefront derives from ([`packages/types/src/market-scope.ts`](./packages/types/src/market-scope.ts)), Redis-cached 120 s. Bounded to open matches and to the market statuses the match page renders: the all-time alternative reads ~17M rows in ~9 s and answers a different question — tabs from fixtures that finished months ago. Per-player Fonbet variants are collapsed in SQL before the DISTINCT (one row per player per market otherwise). Measured on production 2026-09-05: football 28 tabs, ice hockey 15, tennis and basketball 13, an esport 6. **Market pools are what the tab actually holds**, except that every Map tab shares one pool — which kinds are quoted on map 4 right now is an accident of what is live, and an operator has to be able to order Map 5 before a series reaches it; curated tabs (`top` + customs) still draw from every market on the sport, and a market an operator already ordered is always listed even when nothing is live under it. PUT `/admin/fe-settings/markets-order/:sportId/:scope` writes a transactional replace into `fe_market_display_order`. **Market groups (tabs)** are managed at `/admin/fe-settings/markets-order/[sportId]/groups` (create / rename / delete custom groups + reorder ALL tabs), backed by `/admin/fe-settings/market-groups/:sportId` (list / POST / PATCH / DELETE / PUT order). Tab order lives in `fe_market_groups` (migration 0084): tabs WITH a row sort first by `display_order`, the rest keep the default (Top, Match, Map 1..N, then sub-events by Fonbet kind then nesting depth); first configuration seeds anchor rows for every tab the sport actually has, so a new custom group appends at the end. **`/catalog/matches/:id` needed no change for any of this** — it already keyed both the per-tab market ordering and the tab order off the group id, so a saved `fb_*` row reaches the storefront the moment the CHECK lets it exist; what changed there is that the sub-event derivation moved out of the route into the shared module. The storefront's `All` tab is intentionally NOT configurable — it just aggregates the per-scope groups in native order. List endpoints (`/catalog/sports/:slug`, `/catalog/matches`) also include `topMarket` per match so storefront list cards render a [Match \| Top] toggle inline (`apps/web/src/components/match/match-list-tabs.tsx`); custom groups and sub-event tabs are match-detail-only. |
| Admin: team logos | Live (2026-05-06; upload + remove added 2026-05-08) | `/admin/competitors` lists every team with sport/q/missing-logo filters, inline edit of `logo_url` / `brand_color` / `abbreviation`, **plus per-row Upload + Remove buttons** (migration 0040). Same upload pipeline as `/admin/sports`: `POST /admin/competitors/:id/logo` (multipart, SVG / PNG / JPEG / WebP, ≤1 MB) stores bytes in `competitors.logo_data BYTEA` + `competitors.logo_mime TEXT` and stamps `logo_url='/api/competitors/<id>/logo?v=<unix-ms>'`; `DELETE /admin/competitors/:id/logo` clears all three columns in one tx. Public byte-serve at `GET /competitors/:id/logo`. Backed by [`services/api/src/modules/admin/competitors.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/competitors.ts) with audit logging on every mutation. **Auto-resolved logos hot-link directly to Oddin's CDN** — Oddin is our authorised data partner and `cdn.oddin.gg` is built for this. The single resolver, [`packages/db/src/resolve-logos.ts`](./packages/db/src/resolve-logos.ts), is one SQL UPDATE — copy `competitor_profiles.icon_path` (populated by feed-ingester from Oddin's `/v1/sports/{lang}/competitors/{urn}/profile` endpoint, ~1861/2135 profiles populated) onto `competitors.logo_url`. Idempotent; `pnpm --filter @oddzilla/db db:resolve-logos` runs in well under a second. Coverage on the current feed: ~87% of competitors have an `icon_path`; rows without one render the name alone (`TeamMark` draws nothing without a picture since 2026-09-05). Caddy CSP `img-src 'self' data: https:` accommodates the cross-origin `<img>`; `TeamMark` unmounts itself on `<img onError>` so a missing icon never leaves a broken-image placeholder in the row. |
| Admin: sport icons | Live (2026-05-08) | `/admin/sports` per-sport row editor for `logo_url` + `brand_color` (existing) **plus a per-row file upload + Remove logo button** (migration 0039). Upload endpoint `POST /admin/sports/:id/logo` (multipart) accepts SVG / PNG / JPEG / WebP up to 1 MB; stores bytes in `sports.logo_data BYTEA` + `sports.logo_mime TEXT` and stamps `logo_url='/api/sports/<slug>/logo?v=<unix-ms>'` so the storefront's existing `<img>` chain (`SportGlyph` → `useSportLogo` → bundled SVG → `FallbackGlyph`) picks the upload up automatically and re-uploads bust the browser cache via the `?v` param. `DELETE /admin/sports/:id/logo` clears bytes + URL in one tx. PATCH that overwrites `logo_url` with anything other than the byte-serve URL also wipes `logo_data` / `logo_mime` so bytes can't go orphan. Public byte-serve at `GET /sports/:slug/logo` ([`services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts)) returns the bytes with `cache-control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`. PNG/JPEG are stored as-is — true raster→SVG vectorisation is lossy and out of scope; admins should upload SVG directly when they have the source. |

Backed by [`services/api/src/modules/admin/tournaments.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/tournaments.ts): list + patch + multipart upload + delete + sport-filter dropdown source. Public byte-serve at `GET /tournaments/:id/logo`. | Server security hardening | Live | Non-root inside every container (`node:1000` for TS, `app:100` for Go); `cap_drop: ALL` + `no-new-privileges:true` on every service in `docker-compose.yml`; Caddy keeps only `NET_BIND_SERVICE` (PR #130). Per-service `mem_limit` budgeted for the 4 GB CPX22 (postgres 1G, web 640M, api 512M, ws-gateway 256M, Go workers 320M, redis 320M, caddy 96M). Per-service `json-file` log rotation (10 MiB × 5 files) closes the disk-full repeat surface from `project_disk_full_incident`. SSH `PasswordAuthentication no`, `PermitRootLogin no`, `MaxAuthTries 3`, `X11Forwarding no`; repo dir `chmod 750`. **Nonce-based CSP** emitted by Next.js middleware (per-request nonce passed to the inline theme-boot script via `headers().get("x-csp-nonce")`); Caddy's static CSP removed. HSTS now has `preload`. `NODE_ENV=production` on server so auth cookies get `Secure` flag. `docker-compose.override.yml` renamed to `docker-compose.dev.yml` so a forgotten `-f` in production cannot auto-mount the source tree. `fail2ban` was installed briefly then removed (kept banning legit operator connections — password auth off makes brute force impossible anyway, see `project_security_hardening.md` memory). |
| Postgres backups | Live | `/usr/local/bin/oddzilla-pg-backup` (from `infra/hetzner/backup/pg_backup.sh`) runs daily at 03:00 UTC via root cron, `docker exec` into postgres container, writes `/var/backups/oddzilla/*.sql.gz` (root:team 640). **Count-based retention `RETENTION_COUNT` (default 2, lowered from 4 on 2026-06-17)**; prunes BEFORE the dump + atomic `.part` rename so a full disk can't block rotation or leave a truncated dump. PR #130 hardening: reads only `POSTGRES_USER` / `POSTGRES_DB` / `BACKUP_GPG_RECIPIENT` from `.env` (no full source). **The password never touches the host** (2026-09-03): every script that runs `pg_dump` / `psql` in the postgres container — this one, the pre-deploy `infra/deploy/dump-db.sh`, `audit_chain_check.sh`, `odds_retention.sh`, `settlements_retention.sh` — goes through `docker exec <container> sh -c 'export PGPASSWORD="$POSTGRES_PASSWORD"; exec …' sh <user> <db>` so `PGPASSWORD` is read from the container's own environment; the earlier `docker exec -e PGPASSWORD=<value>` form put the real password into `ps` for every local user and into the `sudo` journal line for the duration of each run. Never reintroduce `-e PGPASSWORD=`. `BACKUP_GPG_RECIPIENT` → each dump GPG-encrypted (`.sql.gz.gpg`). **Off-host copy wired 2026-08-26**: operator workstation Task Scheduler job `OddzillaBackupPull` pulls the newest dump daily (sha256-verified, keeps 7 local); the on-box cron pins `RETENTION_COUNT=1`. |
| odds_history retention | Live (2026-06-17; partition model since 2026-08-26) | `/usr/local/bin/oddzilla-odds-retention` (from [`infra/hetzner/backup/odds_retention.sh`](./infra/hetzner/backup/odds_retention.sh)), root cron `30 3 * * *`. Since the 2026-08-26 conversion the table runs on daily dated partitions (`odds_history_pYYYYMMDD` + safety DEFAULT `odds_history_default3`): the cron pre-creates partitions 7 days ahead and DETACH CONCURRENTLY + DROPs those older than `ODDS_RETENTION_DAYS` (default **35**; admin charts look back 30, ZillaTips reads the permanent `prematch_odds` snapshot). Partition DROP returns space to the OS instantly — zero bloat, no high-water, no future reclaims. History: single catch-all DEFAULT + batched DELETE until 2026-08-26 (heap plateaued ~60 GB, never shrank); one-time reclaims ran 2026-07-02 (95%→50%) and 2026-08-26 (export→drop→restore swap, window 45→35, 90%→55%). See docs/OPERATIONS.md. |
| Published odds margin | Active but **0 bp globally** | `odds_config` row `scope='global'` has `payback_margin_bp=0` because Oddin already ships odds with margin baked in. `applyMargin()` divides `raw / (1 + bp/10000)` and renders at up to 4dp with trailing zeros trimmed to a ≥2dp minimum (post-2026-05-25; was floor-truncate to 2dp + 1.01 floor before). No low-side floor on the PUBLISHED price — the storefront shows whatever Oddin sends, including 1.003 or a flat 1.00. **Two things follow from that, both fixed 2026-08-27:** (1) the storefront must render through [`formatOddsDisplay`](./packages/types/src/odds.ts), not `toFixed(2)` — `OddButton`, the compact match-row button, and the slip's per-leg odds all used a fixed 2dp, which printed a genuine 1.003 as "1.00" (a price that doesn't exist, and indistinguishable from a real 1.0000); (2) placement rejects any leg whose authoritative price is `<= 1` with `outcome_odds_too_low` ([`bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts)) — a 1.00 leg returns exactly the stake on a win while carrying full loss/void risk, contributes nothing to a combo's payout, and a non-zero `payback_margin_bp` on any scope divides near-1.0 odds *below* 1.0 (`publisher_test.go` pins "5% margin on 1.003" at `0.9552`). The prior guard only rejected `<= 0`. **Operator decision (2026-08-27): sub-1.01 odds are first-class — no floor, no hiding.** A price of 1.003 displays and places normally end-to-end (`NUMERIC(10,4)` storage, `multiplyMicroByOdds` scales by 10 000, Go `SinglePayout` floors `stake × eff` — a 10 OZ bet at 1.003 pays 10.03). The floor sits at exactly **1.00 and below**, which is *displayed but not offered*: [`isBettableOdds`](./packages/types/src/odds.ts) (`odds > 1`) greys the cell to an em dash in both `OddButton` and the compact match-row button, the same treatment a suspended outcome gets. Deliberately NOT hidden and NOT floored upward — flooring to 1.01 would be a negative-margin quote and would undo `a9bcf44`. Keep `isBettableOdds` and the placement guard in step: the UI must never offer a price `POST /bets` would refuse. Oddin does not appear to quote 1.00 in practice, so this is a defensive floor — but a non-zero `payback_margin_bp` makes sub-1.0 publishing reachable, which is what it really guards. Admin `/admin/margins` still works for per-sport/tournament/market-type overrides when needed. |
| Wallet HD address derivation | Live | Done by the isolated [`services/signer`](./services/signer/) Go service. The API talks to it over a Unix socket on a tmpfs volume that only those two containers mount. The signer reads `HD_MASTER_MNEMONIC` once at boot and `os.Unsetenv`s it; no other process holds the secret. `cap_drop: ALL`, `no_new_privileges`, `read_only: true`, `mem_limit: 64m`. The API has no offline derivation fallback — `SIGNER_SOCKET_PATH` is required. Parity test pins ETH idx=0 to the canonical 0x9858EfFD232B4033E47d90003D41EC34EcaEda94 from the BIP44 abandon-mnemonic vector. |
| Wallet deposit scanners | Live, gated on RPC URLs | Boots idle if `TRON_RPC_URL` / `ETH_RPC_URL` absent. **Reorg-safe (PR #130)**: ETH scanner captures `blockHash` per Transfer log into `deposits.block_hash`; processor calls `Scanner.VerifyDeposit(dep)` before crediting (`eth_getBlockByNumber` lookup, compares hash). On divergence the row flips to `status='orphaned'` instead of crediting. ETH client also re-verifies log `Address` per row (defends against an RPC endpoint that ignores the address filter) and hard-errors on missing `0x` prefix in `Data` (the previous behaviour silently parsed as 0 and advanced the cursor — losing real Transfers). Tron `normalizeTronAddressOK` returns `(_, false)` on unrecognised shapes; scanner aborts the batch (cursor stays put) so a deposit cannot be quietly dropped. TRC20 `event_index` captured so multi-Transfer txs no longer collide on `(network, tx_hash, log_index=0)`. |
| Wrong-token deposit visibility | Live (2026-05-13, migration 0048) | Two complementary surfaces for "user sent the wrong coin to the USDC receive address". (1) Intent path: when a user pastes the tx hash of a non-USDC Transfer, `Inspect()` in [`services/wallet-watcher/internal/ethereum/scanner.go`](./services/wallet-watcher/internal/ethereum/scanner.go) does a second pass for any Transfer-to-receive-address from a non-USDC contract and the processor routes through `RejectIntentWrongToken` which stamps `detected_token_contract` + `detected_token_amount_raw` (uint256, NUMERIC(78,0) — decimals are unknown at insert time) on the rejected intent. `failure_reason='wrong_token'`. (2) Wider scan: `DiscoverIncoming` now runs a SECOND `eth_getLogs` (`GetAllTransfersTo` — no `address` filter, only the recipient topic) over the same block range as the USDC scan. Every non-USDC Transfer becomes an `unattributed_deposits` row keyed by `(network, tx_hash, log_index)`. Token symbol + decimals are best-effort `eth_call` enrichment (`symbol()` / `decimals()` selectors, ABI dynamic-string decoder, per-contract in-memory cache); failed enrichment leaves NULL columns and the admin UI renders the raw uint256 amount plus the contract address. Cursor only advances after both scans succeed, so a transient `eth_getLogs` failure idempotently retries next tick (INSERTs are unique-key guarded). Dedup: inserting an unattributed row is skipped when a `deposit_intent` already represents the same `(network, tx_hash)`; conversely, rejecting an intent as wrong_token opportunistically acks any matching unattributed row inside the same transaction. Admin surface: `/admin/deposits` gains a tab strip (All / Pending / Confirming / Credited / Rejected / **Wrong Token** / **Unattributed**), unack-only by default on the alert tabs with an explicit Acknowledged / Both toggle. Acknowledge endpoints `POST /admin/deposits/:id/acknowledge` (wrong_token intents) and `POST /admin/deposits/unattributed/:id/acknowledge` both write `admin_audit_log` and accept `{undo: true}` for re-opening. The admin sidebar polls `GET /admin/deposits/alert-counts` every 60 s and renders a red numeric badge on the Deposits entry (red dot when collapsed). |
| Wallet withdrawal on-chain submission | **Manual** | Admin marks-submitted with tx hash from external signer/wallet. Pre-launch needs a dedicated signer container |
| News scraper | **Removed** | Service + `news_articles` table deleted via migration 0003; no longer in scope |

| Cash-out | Live | Migration 0015 adds `cashout_config`, `cashouts`, probability columns on `market_outcomes` / `odds_history` / `ticket_selections`, and a `cashout` value on `wallet_tx_type`. Algorithm is Sportradar §2.1.1 (`stake × ticketOdds × Π(legProb)`) with optional §2.1.2 deduction ladder; full-stake prematch window is configurable per-scope. Oddin already populates `probabilities="0.368"` on every active outcome (verified on the integration broker 2026-04-28); engine falls back to `1/oddsCurrent` when the attribute is missing. Endpoints: `GET /tickets/:id/cashout/quote`, `POST /tickets/:id/cashout`, plus `/admin/cashout-config` cascade. Frontend: [`apps/web/src/app/(main)/bets/cashout-panel.tsx`](./apps/web/src/app/(main)/bets/cashout-panel.tsx) polls every 2s and surfaces an accept dialog. Settlement is unaffected — `maybeSettleTicket` already gates on `t.Status == "accepted"`, so `cashed_out` tickets are never re-paid. |
| Community achievements + AI seed bettor PnL filter (Phase 10.4) | Live (2026-05-06) | Migration 0029 adds `achievement_definitions` + `user_achievements` (composite PK on `(user_id, achievement_id)` for idempotent unlocks). Five starter badges seeded inline: `first_win`, `combo_5`, `odds_20`, `payout_100x`, `streak_10`. Currency-agnostic predicates evaluated against `community_tickets` aggregates by [`EvaluateAchievements`](./services/settlement/internal/store/store.go) (Go) and [`evaluateAchievements`](./services/api/src/modules/community/achievements.ts) (TS), called immediately after every projection write — settle path, cashout path, admin backfill. `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` makes re-evaluation a no-op; rollback never revokes (achievements are facts about user history). Public profile API now returns the unlock list inline; storefront `/u/[nickname]` renders an Achievements grid with lucide icons. AI seed bettor accounts (`users.is_ai = true`) are now excluded from `/admin/stats/{kpis,pnl-by-day,big-wins}` per Decision D2 — their volume settles through real ledger rows but isn't real revenue. The bet generator (the actual placement loop) is a 10.4b follow-up. |
| Community scoring + Best Wins + Copy (Phase 10.3) | Live (2026-05-06) | Score formula written into the projection upsert SQL, frozen at settlement time. Components: Inspiration 25 (`log10(payout/stake)`, capped, only on wins), Odds 15 (`log(total_odds)/log(20)`, capped), Reputation 15 (user's prior win-rate in this currency from `community_tickets`, snapshot at `settled_at`), Copyability 15 (reserved for 10.4, 0 today). Recency (30 pts) is applied at QUERY time — `?sort=best` filters `settled_at >= now() - interval '7 days'` and uses the existing `(score DESC, settled_at DESC)` index. The stored score is time-invariant; no cron recompute needed. `GET /community/feed?sort=best` powered by the same endpoint as recent. `POST /community/copy/:communityTicketId` returns a slip-ready selection list (matchId/marketId/outcomeId/odds + display labels + sportSlug + per-leg `available` flag); the web client adds them to the existing bet-slip via [`useBetSlip().add()`](./apps/web/src/lib/bet-slip.tsx) and opens the rail for confirmation. POST /bets re-validates everything at placement, so drift between copy-click and confirm surfaces there. UI: Recent / Best Wins tab strip on `/community`; Copy this bet button on every `CommunityTicketCard`. |

Both paths share the same upsert SQL (apply-once on `ticket_id UNIQUE` + `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`). Sidebar: top-level "Community" entry → /community (was previously aliased to settings). Profile stats refactored to aggregate from `community_tickets` (settled / wins / win rate / ROI per currency). Admin recovery: `POST /admin/community/backfill` sweeps any miss in 500-row batches; idempotent. |

| Security audit (6-PR pass) | Live (2026-05-06, PR #130) | All Critical + High + selected Medium findings from the full-project audit landed in one merge: 4-eyes withdrawal flow, tamper-evident audit log (SHA-256 hash chain via BEFORE INSERT trigger + `admin_audit_chain_check()` verifier), reorg-safe deposit credits, ETH/Tron scanner hardening, refresh-token family + replay detection, per-request session-revocation cache, JWT audience claim, Origin-based CSRF plugin, login-timing equalisation, argon2id bumped to 2026 OWASP minimum, password change rate-limited + immediate other-device session kill, web container decoupled from `JWT_SECRET`, nonce-based CSP, javascript: URL filter on Oddin streams, status-code allow-list `{0,1,4,5}`, REST body cap (`http.MaxBytesReader`), name length cap on auto-mapper, container `cap_drop` + `no-new-privileges` + `mem_limit` + log rotation, encrypted backup option, settlement payload-hash canonicalisation (drops raw bytes — closes whitespace-replay duplicate-payout path), cashout drift floor in bigint (was 32-bit truncating for stakes > ~2147 USDT), bulk-logos `eq()` instead of `ilike()`, `/admin/feed/recovery` rate-limited 3/hr, admin-self-edit guard extended to `globalLimitMicro` + `betDelaySeconds`, typed errors in admin config (no more 500-on-validation-failure). See PR description for the full list. |

Currencies hard-coded in [`packages/types/src/currencies.ts`](./packages/types/src/currencies.ts). | Oddin Disir widgets (prematch + live) | Live (2026-05-06) | Server-side proxy at `/widgets/*` ([`services/api/src/modules/widgets/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/widgets/routes.ts)) holds the `x-brand-token`, calls Disir's REST per the December 2025 integration doc (`HumanDocs/Oddin.gg Disir - Widgets documentation.docx`), and returns `{url}` for the storefront iframe `src`. Three endpoints: prematch-match, prematch-tournament, live-scoreboard. URLs are 120s Redis-cached so 50 simultaneous viewers hit Disir once. Frontend wraps every iframe in [`DisirWidget`](./apps/web/src/components/widgets/disir-widget.tsx) which subscribes to the doc-defined postMessage events (`LOADED`/`RESIZE`/`DATA`/`CLOSE`/`SCROLL_TOP`) — `RESIZE.height` drives `iframe.style.height`, live widgets stay `display:none` until `DATA: {available: true}` arrives. **Desktop layout**: prematch widget mounts inside the right rail aside (`oz-rail-prematch`) below the bet slip via [`MatchPageContext`](./apps/web/src/lib/match-page-context.tsx); live scoreboard sits below the Twitch / YouTube embed inside [`MatchLiveMedia`](./apps/web/src/components/widgets/match-live-media.tsx). **Mobile (≤1099px)**: rail-embedded prematch hidden; the match page renders [`MatchPrematchMobile`](./apps/web/src/components/widgets/match-prematch-mobile.tsx) (collapsed toggle button under the match info, expands to a full-width iframe above the markets). Live media swaps to a Stream/Stats pill toggle. Sport gating in [`supported-sports.ts`](./apps/web/src/components/widgets/supported-sports.ts) — prematch covers cs2/dota2/lol/valorant/efootball, live covers those + ebasketball/ecricket. CSP `frame-src` whitelists `https://*.oddin.gg`. Domain-whitelisting is a manual step on Oddin's side: every host that embeds a widget must be emailed to `support@oddin.gg` (currently `oddzilla.cc`). |
| Oddin BetBuilder (OBB) — same-match combos | Live (2026-05-07) | Migration 0031 extends `bet_type` with `'betbuilder'`. New `services/api/src/modules/betbuilder/routes.ts` proxies Oddin's gRPC: `GET /betbuilder/match/:id/markets` (eligibility) and `POST /betbuilder/match/:id/quote` (SessionCreate → combined session odds + still-available markets). gRPC client at [`services/api/src/lib/obb-client.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/obb-client.ts) — vendored proto under [`services/api/proto/obb/`](./services/api/proto/obb/). Frontend: a manual toggle on the match page ([`apps/web/src/components/match/betbuilder-toggle.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/match/betbuilder-toggle.tsx)) flips the slip into `mode: "betbuilder"` for that match — `add()` then accepts multiple legs from the same match (default same-match replace is bypassed). Slip rail re-quotes on every leg change and submits `POST /bets` with `betType="betbuilder"` + `betBuilder: {sessionId, expectedOddsX10000, selectionIds}`. Server calls `SessionInfo` to revalidate before debiting; on `invalid` the placement 400s `betbuilder_session_invalid` and the slip refreshes. Settlement path ([`BetBuilderPayout` in payout.go](./services/settlement/internal/settler/payout.go)): all-won → `stake × oddsX10000/10_000` (bet_payout); any leg lost → 0 (bet_payout); any leg voided + non-loss rest → refund stake (bet_refund) since OBB combined odds aren't a product and we can't recompute on partial voids. bet-delay branches on `bet_type='betbuilder'` to skip per-leg drift (the Oddin session combined odds are non-multiplicative; per-leg drift is a meaningless tripwire), but still enforces market + outcome activity. Sport gating mirrors OBB doc Appendix #1 (CS2 / CS2 Duels / Valorant / eFootball / eBasketball). **Disabled until env populated**: `ODDIN_OBB_HOST` empty → `/betbuilder/*` 503s `betbuilder_disabled`, toggle hides on the match page, placement of `betType="betbuilder"` is rejected upfront — same graceful-idle pattern Disir uses. Token defaults to `ODDIN_TOKEN` (Oddin issues a single per-customer token; override with `ODDIN_OBB_TOKEN` only if they cut a separate one). IP allowlist: every environment that calls OBB must be whitelisted via Oddin's dedicated channel before flipping `ODDIN_OBB_HOST` on. |
| Oddin video (Havik player) — first-party match streams | Live, gated on `ODDIN_VIDEO_API_KEY` (2026-08-31) | DRM live video for the matches Oddin carries, played in-page by `@oddin-gg/havik-player` (no iframe). `GET /video/match/:matchId` ([`services/api/src/modules/video/`](./services/api/src/modules/video/)) answers "is there a stream, and what does the browser need" in one round trip; the storefront mounts the player as the first source in the match page's existing stream tab strip, ahead of the fixture's Twitch / YouTube URLs. Availability comes from a 60 s Redis-cached flatten of Oddin's `/v1/catalog` — **not** from probing `/v1/playback/{urn}`, which returns `503 UNAVAILABLE` for uncovered matches and so cannot distinguish "no stream" from "try again". `waitForLive` arms prematch and attaches at go-live with no reload. **Signed-in bettors only** (operator decision 2026-08-31): an anonymous viewer sees the source tab with a "Sign in to watch" card in the player's place — the api withholds the credential, so there is nothing a logged-out client can replay, and [`use-oddin-video.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-oddin-video.ts) keys its fetch on `useSessionUserId()` so logging in swaps the card for a live player without a reload. **The api-key is publishable by design** (`pk_test_…` / `pk_live_…`): Oddin scopes it with a server-side allowed-origin list and the browser SDK needs it in hand to sign its own DRM licence POSTs, so it is served to the page at runtime rather than baked into the web bundle — rotation is an `.env` edit plus `make recreate api`, no storefront rebuild. `oddzilla.cc` is already on Oddin's allowed-origin list (verified 2026-08-31); **every new host that mounts the player needs adding on Oddin's side**, same manual step as the Disir widget domains. **The allow-list is enforced server-side and a MISSING `Origin` fails it** — a plain server-to-server catalog fetch gets `403 origin not allowed`, so the api sends `Origin: https://${FRONTEND_HOST}` explicitly (Node's fetch permits that; browsers don't). This is not CORS and it cost a deploy to find, because every hand-run probe had sent an Origin out of habit; the symptom is video unavailable on every match with `oddin video: catalog rejected our api key or origin` in the api log. CSP gained `connect-src https://*.oddin-video.gg`, `media-src 'self' blob:` and `worker-src 'self' blob:` — playback fails silently without all three. **Graceful-idle**: empty key → 503 `video_disabled`, no player, no tab, storefront byte-identical to before. Activation = set `ODDIN_VIDEO_API_KEY` (+ `ODDIN_VIDEO_BASE_URL=https://feed.oddin-video.gg` for prod), `make recreate api`. |
