| Android client (storefront only) | Live (v0.5.0, **WebView shell**): full storefront via Chromium WebView pointing at `https://oddzilla.cc` + native update flow + FCM scaffolding | [`apps/mobile-android/`](./apps/mobile-android/) — **Pivoted from native Compose rewrite to thin WebView shell on 2026-05-10.** The earlier v0.4.0 native-Compose port (5-tab bottom nav, ported design tokens, native bet-slip / community feed / wallet) was producing a UI that diverged visibly from the responsive web app, and keeping a separate Compose surface in step with the web design was costing more than it returned. Single-Activity host ([`MainActivity.kt`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/MainActivity.kt)) puts a Chromium [`WebViewHost`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/web/WebViewHost.kt) under an [`UpdateGate`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/update/UpdateGate.kt) modal layer; nothing else is on screen. WebView config: JavaScript + DOM storage on, third-party cookies on (so Disir / Twitch / YouTube / Kick / Gjirafa iframes log in), strict `MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW`, UA-token `Oddzilla-Android/<ver>`, `oddzilla.cc` + `*.oddin.gg` + the four streaming hosts stay in-app while every other URL fans out via `Intent.ACTION_VIEW`. Back press goes back in WebView history if possible, otherwise `moveTaskToBack` (keeps the WebView warm for re-launch). Cookies mirror from the WebView's `CookieManager` into [`PersistentCookieJar`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/data/api/CookieStore.kt) on every `onPageFinished` so the OkHttp client used by [`DevicesRepository`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/data/repo/DevicesRepository.kt) (FCM device-register) authenticates against the same session. **REST surface trimmed** to the three `/devices/*` endpoints — auth, catalog, wallet, bets, community, cashout, copy-this-bet, drift-accept, live odds WS, deposit intents, withdrawal requests are all the web app's responsibility now. The retired native sources (`bet/`, `data/repo/{Auth,Bets,Catalog,Community,Wallet}Repository.kt`, `data/ws/`, `nav/`, `ui/screens/`, `ui/components/`, `common/{Money,Snackbar}.kt`) are gone — get them from git history if you ever need a reference Compose port. **FCM push pipeline is fully wired**: server-side outbox + worker (migration `0058_push_notifications_outbox` + [`services/api/src/modules/push/`](./services/api/src/modules/push/)) AND client-side ([`FcmService.kt`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/FcmService.kt) + [`PushBootstrap.kt`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/PushBootstrap.kt), manifest service entry, conditional Google Services Gradle plugin, login-transition register hook in `WebViewHost.onPageFinished`, Android-13+ POST_NOTIFICATIONS prompt in `MainActivity`). The Gradle plugin is applied only when `app/google-services.json` exists so fresh checkouts still build; `BuildConfig.FIREBASE_ENABLED` carries that signal at runtime and `PushBootstrap` no-ops cleanly when false. Activation now reduces to: generate the Firebase service-account JSON on the box (already done), generate `google-services.json` from the Firebase Console and drop at `apps/mobile-android/app/google-services.json` (gitignored), rebuild the APK and ship. See [`fcm/README.md`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/README.md). **CI**: [`.github/workflows/android.yml`](./.github/workflows/android.yml) — push to main builds debug APK as a workflow artifact; `workflow_dispatch -release=true` signs with keystore restored from GH secrets and scps the result to `/srv/oddzilla-apk/`. Release flow: `apps/mobile-android/scripts/release.ps1` (or `.sh`, or the GH Actions release dispatch) builds a signed APK, scps to `/srv/oddzilla-apk/oddzilla-<version>.apk`, updates `version.json`. Caddy reverse-proxies the dir at `https://oddzilla.cc/app/*` with `Cache-Control: no-store` on the JSON manifest so update prompts propagate within minutes. |
| ZillaTips — per-market historical-ROI hint | Live (2026-05-11) | Migration 0047 adds `matches.live_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ` + `market_outcomes.prematch_odds NUMERIC(10,4)`, **and backfills both inside the migration** — `live_started_at` from `scheduled_at`, and `prematch_odds` from the most recent `odds_history` tick before `live_started_at` for every match still within the 90-day partition retention window. Capture going forward: feed-ingester's `UpdateMatchStatus` ([`store/catalog.go`](./services/feed-ingester/internal/store/catalog.go)) detects the first not_started→live transition via a CTE that COALESCEs `live_started_at = NOW()` and copies `published_odds → prematch_odds` for every outcome on the match in one statement (IS NULL guards = idempotent under recovery replay). The new partial-composite indexes `matches_home_team_recency_idx` / `matches_away_team_recency_idx` let the lookback subquery index-scan the top-5 per team without a sort. API: `GET /catalog/matches/:matchId/zillatips` ([`zillatips/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/zillatips/routes.ts)) runs one parameterised CTE that (1) enumerates the match's active markets × outcomes, (2) decides which team(s) each outcome relates to — `provider_market_id IN (1,4)` gets the home/away team-of-interest gate; everything else is symmetric — (3) pulls each team's last 5 closed matches with the same `(provider_market_id, specifiers_hash)` signature, (4) translates the outcome via 1↔2 swap when the team's home/away role differs across matches, (5) hydrates `prematch_odds` + `result` and aggregates flat-stake ROI: `Σ(won? odds-1 : lost? -1 : half_won? (odds-1)/2 : half_lost? -0.5) / rated_count`. Result filtered to `roi ≥ ZILLATIP_MIN_ROI` (20%). Redis caches the response 5 min per match. Frontend: [`use-zillatips.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-zillatips.ts) hook fetches once on match-page mount; [`zillatips-widget.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/match/zillatips-widget.tsx) renders a chip badge next to the market name with three visual tiers (`ZILLATIP_TIER_GLOW`=100% / `ZILLATIP_TIER_FIRE`=300%, all thresholds in [`packages/types/src/zillatips.ts`](./packages/types/src/zillatips.ts)). Hover/click opens a popover with one section per (outcome, team-role) tip: focused team + per-leg ROI + 5 opponent chips colour-coded won/lost/void. Matches >90 days old (whose odds_history partitions have been pruned) stay NULL and degrade gracefully — those legs are shown but excluded from the ROI denominator. |
| Inbound email + backoffice inbox | Live, gated on DNS swap (2026-05-21) | Migration `0074_email_inbound` adds `email_threads`, `email_inbound`, and extends `email_outbox` with `thread_id` / `provider_message_id` / `text_body` / `html_body` / `in_reply_to`. Outbound participates in threads through two new kinds (`admin_outbound`, `admin_reply`); the existing LISTEN/NOTIFY worker dispatches them and bumps thread counters on commit. Inbound path: external SMTP → host port 25 → [`services/mail-receiver`](./services/mail-receiver/) (Go binary, `emersion/go-smtp`) on the Hetzner box → POST to api's `/webhooks/sendgrid-inbound/<SENDGRID_INBOUND_SECRET>` on the docker network → multipart parser → thread resolution (RFC 5322 In-Reply-To → References → normalised subject, last 30 days) → idempotent insert on Message-ID → bump `last_inbound_at` + `inbound_count`. **Why self-hosted, not SendGrid**: Twilio rejected the operator's SendGrid signup citing regional service requirements; Hetzner-hosted Postfix-equivalent gives us a region/industry-neutral inbound path no third party can revoke. The api-side webhook keeps the `sendgrid-inbound` slug because the payload shape is identical (mail-receiver mimics SendGrid Inbound Parse exactly) — when we eventually swap providers, the api code doesn't change. **Attachment file contents are not persisted** — metadata only (filename / type / size). CSRF is skipped for `/webhooks/*` since webhooks are server-to-server with their own auth (path secret, constant-time compared, 404 on miss). Admin: `/admin/emails` inbox list with filter tabs (Inbox / Unread / Archived / All), search, cursor pagination; `/admin/emails/[id]` thread view with reply form + archive toggle + auto-mark-read; `/admin/emails/new` compose. Sidebar **Emails** entry under Operations polls `/admin/emails/unread-count` every 60 s and renders the thread count as a badge. **Graceful-idle**: when `SENDGRID_INBOUND_SECRET` is unset the webhook 503s `inbound_disabled` AND the mail-receiver fails to boot (required env var), surfacing the misconfig immediately. Activation = generate `SENDGRID_INBOUND_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 24)`, paste into `.env`, ensure DNS is `mail.oddzilla.cc A 178.104.174.24` + `oddzilla.cc MX 10 mail.oddzilla.cc.`, `make deploy`. No external account, no provider signup. Operator owns the entire inbound path on their own Hetzner box. |
| Transactional email (verify + reset) | Live, gated on provider credentials (2026-05-21) | Migration `0073_email` adds `users.email_verified_at`, `email_outbox` (durable queue), `email_verification_tokens`, `password_reset_tokens` (single-use, hash-only). Signup wires `enqueueVerifyEmail` into the same tx as the user-insert; the email never goes out unless the user row commits. Four new auth routes: `POST /auth/verify-email`, `POST /auth/resend-verification` (auth-required, 3/min), `POST /auth/forgot-password` (always 200, namespace-scoped by host so a storefront reset can't trigger the admin row that shares the email), `POST /auth/reset-password` (revokes every session + clears cookies on success). Worker `startEmailOutboxWorker` ([`services/api/src/modules/email/worker.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/email/worker.ts)) LISTENs on `email_outbox`, sweeps every 30 s with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, dispatches via the Resend HTTP API; `MAX_ATTEMPTS=5` then force-marks sent with last_error preserved. Token model: 256-bit `randomBytes(32)` base64url'd in the email; only sha256 lives in the DB so a table exfiltration leaks no usable values. 24 h TTL for verify, 30 min for reset; consumption stamps `used_at` + burns every other unused token for the same user. Storefront: dismissible banner in the (main) layout while `emailVerifiedAt IS NULL`, /verify-email landing page that POSTs the token + refreshes the SSR shell on success, /forgot-password form, /reset-password form. i18n keys added to all 6 locales (en/es/pt/ru/cs/hr). Provider-agnostic by design: the `EmailClient` interface in [`client.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/email/client.ts) supports `resend\|sendgrid\|ses\|smtp` — only `resend` ships today, swapping is a one-file change. **Graceful-idle**: when `EMAIL_PROVIDER_TOKEN` is unset the worker still drains rows with `last_error='email_disabled'` so the table stays bounded. Activation = `EMAIL_PROVIDER_TOKEN=re_xxx` (Resend free tier: 3K/month, https://resend.com), `EMAIL_FROM=noreply@oddzilla.cc`, verify the send-domain in Resend's dashboard, `make recreate api`. Storefront link copy lives in [`apps/web/messages/*.json`](./apps/web/messages/) under the `auth` namespace; the public link base URL falls back to `https://${FRONTEND_HOST}` when `EMAIL_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` is unset. |
| FCM push pipeline (bet wins) | Live, gated on Firebase credentials (2026-05-16) | Migration `0058_push_notifications_outbox` adds the durable outbox table. Settlement (Go) `maybeSettleTicket` writes a `bet_won` row inside the same tx as `SettleTicket` whenever `payout > stake_micro` (full wins + half-wins), then fires `NOTIFY push_outbox` after the chunk tx commits. The api service runs [`startPushOutboxWorker`](./services/api/src/modules/push/worker.ts) — LISTENs on the channel + sweeps every 30 s with `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, joins to `user_devices` (active rows only), and dispatches via Firebase Admin SDK `sendEachForMulticast`. Dead FCM tokens (`messaging/invalid-argument`, `messaging/registration-token-not-registered` …) get soft-revoked on `user_devices`. Transient FCM errors bump `attempts` + `last_error`; after `MAX_ATTEMPTS=5` the row is force-marked sent so the worker stops thrashing. **Graceful-idle**: when `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PATH` is unset OR the JSON file is missing inside the api container, the worker still drains the queue but stamps each row `sent_at=NOW(), last_error='firebase_disabled'` — the table size stays bounded and the operator activates real sending later just by mounting credentials + restarting api. Compose mounts the directory `/srv/oddzilla-firebase` (not the file) so a missing service-account doesn't abort `docker compose up`. Android client side is wired in code: [`FcmService.kt`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/FcmService.kt) + [`PushBootstrap.kt`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/PushBootstrap.kt), manifest service entry, conditional Google Services Gradle plugin gated on `app/google-services.json` presence so fresh checkouts still build, `BuildConfig.FIREBASE_ENABLED` flag read by `PushBootstrap` to no-op cleanly when the JSON is absent, login-transition register/unregister fired from `WebViewHost.onPageFinished`, Android-13+ `POST_NOTIFICATIONS` runtime prompt in `MainActivity`. Activation reduces to a single file drop — `apps/mobile-android/app/google-services.json` from the Firebase Console — then rebuild and ship the APK. |
| Performance monitoring (admin /monitoring) | Live (2026-05-09) | New `services/metrics-collector` Go sidecar (distroless static, runs as nobody, `cap_drop: ALL`, `read_only: true`, `mem_limit: 64m`) exposes `GET /snapshot` on the docker default network at port 9090 — host CPU%/load/memory/swap/disk via `/host/proc` + statvfs(`/host-root`), plus container list via `/var/run/docker.sock:ro`. The api proxies it for the live KPI tiles (`/admin/monitoring/snapshot`, polled every 5 s) and runs a 60 s background sampler that writes compact rows (disk/mem/swap/cpu/load1/containers) to a Redis sorted set (`monitoring:samples`, 24 h retention) under a NX EX 90 lock so a future multi-api deploy still produces one sample per minute. Frontend at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/monitoring/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/monitoring/) — KPI grid (disk / memory / swap / CPU / load / containers / uptime / free disk), 24 h SVG line charts (disk / memory / load), container health table. Severity thresholds tuned to box incident history: disk >70/85%, memory >75/90%, swap >25/50%, load >cpu_count / >2x cpu_count. Banner reflects max severity across host metrics + container health (anything restarting or unhealthy = red). Graceful-idle: when the collector is down, snapshot returns 503 `monitoring_unavailable` and the page renders "metrics unavailable" without crashing. Built after the 2026-05-09 disk-full repeat outage to surface the same kind of pressure earlier. Doesn't replace the future Prometheus + Grafana row below — this is an in-product operator view, not a metrics warehouse. |
| ZillaPass (quest scaffold + stage progression + four day-sets + no-reset progress) | Live (2026-05-17, sets 3+4 added 2026-05-19, no-reset 2026-05-21) | Migration `0060_zillapass` adds `zillapass_tasks` (admin catalog) + `zillapass_user_progress` (per-user, per-period) + `zillapass_user_state` (level / xp / streak). Migration `0061_zillapass_set_1` adds `progress_state JSONB` to `zillapass_user_progress` (for set-shaped predicates that dedupe) and seeds the **day-1 set**: `profile-complete` (season; completes when `users.nickname IS NOT NULL AND users.avatar_template_id IS NOT NULL`), `open-5-sports` (daily; distinct-sport-count from /sport/:slug page mounts), `open-5-matches-different-sports` (daily; distinct sports across opened matches). Migration `0062_zillapass_set_2` seeds the **day-2 set** (singles-only, currency-agnostic counters): `place-5-prematch-bets`, `place-5-live-bets`, `change-market-tab-10`. Migration `0064_zillapass_stages` introduces **per-user stage progression**: tasks gain `set_number`, user state gains `current_set_number` + `last_set_completed_date`. Migration `0068_zillapass_set_3` seeds the **day-3 set** (three daily sport-specific tasks — `place-5-bets-cs2` / `place-5-bets-dota2` / `place-5-bets-lol`, predicate keys `bets_sport_<slug>`; a ticket counts only when EVERY leg's match shares that sport, so singles trivially qualify and pure-sport multibets count once). Migration `0069_zillapass_set_4` seeds the **day-4 set** (three daily product-specific tasks — `place-5-combos` / `place-5-tiples` / `place-5-tippots`, predicate keys `bets_product_<kind>` keyed off `tickets.bet_type`; BetBuilder is intentionally excluded — its all-legs-same-match shape would duplicate set 3's all-legs-same-sport semantics for the special case). A user only sees tasks where `task.set_number = state.current_set_number`; on completing every active task in their current set, the writer stamps `last_set_completed_date = today` (UTC); on the next /zillapass/me read where today > stamp, the reader advances `current_set_number += 1` and clears the stamp. Net effect: slow users stay on day-1 until they finish, fast users advance at most one set per UTC day. Power users past the last seeded set get `tasks: []` and the top-bar chip self-hides until an admin seeds set N+1. API: `GET /zillapass/me` does the stage advancement check at top, then filters tasks to `set_number = current_set_number`; `POST /zillapass/track` accepts `{event: "sport_view" \| "match_view" \| "market_tab_change", ...}`; `/admin/zillapass/tasks` is full CRUD (now including `setNumber`) with audit logging; **`PUT /admin/users/:id/zillapass-stage`** is a debug-only override that lets an admin force `current_set_number` and/or `last_set_completed_date` for any user — surfaced as a card on `/admin/users/[id]` with Reset + "Stamp yesterday (advances next read)" shortcuts (audit-logged). Predicate writers in [`services/api/src/modules/zillapass/writer.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/zillapass/writer.ts) — `nudgeProfileComplete` re-reads the user row from the nickname-PATCH + avatar-PUT handlers, `nudgeSportViewed` / `nudgeMatchViewed` maintain set-shaped progress under `SELECT FOR UPDATE`, `nudgeBetPlaced` runs a single join through `tickets → ticket_selections → markets → matches → tournaments → categories → sports` and fans out to `bets_prematch`/`bets_live` (singles only), `bets_sport_<slug>` (every leg shares the sport), and `bets_product_<combo|tiple|tippot>` (keyed off `tickets.bet_type`), `nudgeMarketTabChange` increments via atomic `LEAST(current_count + 1, target_count)` ON CONFLICT. Every nudge ends with `maybeStampSetCompletion(app, userId)` which short-circuits if the stamp is already set, otherwise checks "is every active task in current set complete today?" and stamps if so. All writers best-effort: errors logged + swallowed so engagement nudges never break the originating action. Storefront: signed-in-only chip in the shell-search row showing `completed/total` + a mini progress bar (self-hides when total=0), click for per-task popover with an "Open ZillaPass" button to `/zillapass`. Profile dropdown carries a ZillaPass entry. Admin lives at `/admin/zillapass` (Storefront group). Frontend trackers [`SportViewTracker` / `MatchViewTracker` / `useMarketTabChangeTracker`](./apps/web/src/lib/zillapass-track.tsx) — page-mount components dedupe per session, tab-change hook fires only on actual scope changes (re-clicks no-op client-side, programmatic fallbacks bypass the hook). **Still scaffold-only** at the unlock pipeline: `reward_kind` + `reward_payload jsonb` are stored on tasks but nothing reads them yet. **Migration `0073_zillapass_no_daily_reset` (2026-05-21)** removes the per-day reset semantics — every shipped task is now `season`-period, so progress accumulates across days under the fixed `'2026-01-01'` anchor key. A bettor at 3/5 at end-of-day still has 3/5 tomorrow; completing a task is a permanent fact about the user, not a per-day pass. Stage progression is unchanged — the writer's `maybeStampSetCompletion` still fires from every nudge and stamps `last_set_completed_date` when every active task in the current set is complete, and the reader still advances `current_set_number += 1` on the next UTC day. (Edge case: a user whose carried-over progress crosses set-completion at migration time but doesn't fire a nudge for some days will stay on their current set until the next nudge — the stamping is writer-driven, not reader-driven. Self-healing on the first action.) User-facing card drops the small "daily" / "season" period chip — with no resets the label was misleading. Admin form defaults new tasks to `season`; daily / weekly remain available in the dropdown for operators who explicitly want reset semantics on a future task. |
| Per-bettor odds adjustment | Live (2026-05-19; low floor removed 2026-05-25, re-added at 1.001 on 2026-05-27) | Migration `0070_bettor_odds_adjustment` adds `bettor_odds_adjustment_config` keyed by `(user_id, scope, ref-id)` with one typed FK per scope tier and partial unique indexes per (scope, ref). Cascade at read time: `match > tournament > sport > global`, first non-NULL wins. Apply: `published × (1 + bp/10000)`, clamped to `[1.001, 1/probability]`. The high clamp is the operator-asked fair-odds ceiling so a positive bp can't accidentally hand the bettor +EV money; the low floor (`ADJUSTED_ODDS_FLOOR=1.001`, mirrored byte-identically in `services/bet-delay/internal/worker/adjust.go` and `services/ws-gateway/src/bettor-adjustment.ts`) catches the geometric case where a small negative bp on near-1.0 raw odds (e.g. 1.02 × 0.97 ≈ 0.99) would otherwise produce a sub-1.0 price — meaning a winning ticket pays less than the stake, which is nonsense as a bettor-facing display. Range: signed bp in `[-9000, 9000]` (±90%). Plumbed into [`/catalog/sports/:slug`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts), [`/catalog/matches`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts), [`/catalog/matches/:id`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts) for SSR; the Top market helper threads the same formatter so list-card inline odds + Top tab + match-detail outcomes all agree per-bettor. `/bets` placement (services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts) re-derives the bettor's adjusted current price and uses it as the drift reference so the slip's captured (adjusted) price + server-side recomputed (adjusted) price line up. The bet-delay worker ([`services/bet-delay/internal/worker/`](./services/bet-delay/internal/worker/) — `LoadBettorAdjustment` in `store.go`, `applyBettorAdjustment` in `adjust.go`, branch in `evaluate()`) does the same on every re-validation, including the `accept_odds_changes=true` re-pricing path so the new payout reflects the adjusted prices. **Live WS-side adjustment** is wired through too: `odds-publisher` extends `OutboundPayload` with `sportId` + `tournamentId`; the ws-gateway holds an in-memory `Map<userId, Cascade>` loaded once on each authed upgrade and dropped on the last socket close; the dispatch loop applies bp per subscriber with a zero-allocation fast path (bp=0) and per-bp memoisation across subscribers of the same match. Invalidation rides a single Redis pub/sub channel `bettor_adjustment_invalidated` — every admin mutation publishes the affected userId, ws-gateway drops + eager-reloads if the user is still connected. Tiple / tippot / betbuilder explicitly skip the cascade — those products anchor pricing on probabilities or OBB session combined odds, where a per-leg multiplier is meaningless. Admin: routes in [`services/api/src/modules/admin/odds-adjustment.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/odds-adjustment.ts) (summary / sports tree / tournaments tree / matches tree / scope CRUD; every mutation audit-logged + publishes invalidation). UI at [`/admin/users/[id]/odds-adjustment`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/odds-adjustment/) — sport → tournament → match tree with −1% / −0.5% / +0.5% / +1% stepper buttons; user-detail page picks up a summary card. |
| RiskZilla bot controls (placement intent + velocity + behaviour scoring) | Live (2026-09-04) | Migrations `0096`–`0098`. Answer to "can we require a real mouse click to place a bet?" is no — anything a browser sends a script can send — so the controls make automation unprofitable and visible instead. `POST /bets/intent` issues an HMAC token bound to the slip's selection set; `POST /bets` requires it (operator off-switch `intent_required`), enforces `min_human_ms` (600) between quote and place, and records `tickets.quote_to_place_ms`. Per-account velocity caps (12 bets / 10 distinct matches per minute at RS 1.0, scaled by risk score) reject as `rejected_velocity` for every currency. A 5-minute sweeper scores signed-in analytics sessions for automation signals (pointer straightness / speed variation / heading entropy / clicks without approach / click rhythm), rolls them up per bettor, and raises an alert past the configured threshold. Admin: new **Bot controls** tab (config + alerts), Automation signals panel + banner on the bettor profile, Bot column on the bettors list, alerts KPI on the dashboard. Unit tests cover the token, the scoring maths and the velocity cap math; the end-to-end path has not yet been exercised against a running stack. |
| ZillaAGI tournament risk-tier review | Live (2026-09-05) | Migrations `0106` + `0107`. **First production run drained the entire backlog in one 6.9-minute pass**: 1 212 eligible, 1 211 assigned, 1 undecided, 0 errors, 49 model calls, 1 ceiling clamp (RLCS Worlds T2 → T3 on Rocket League's ceiling). Two operator tightenings landed straight after and were applied retroactively by 0107: a standing **+1** on every ZAGI verdict (so ZAGI can never assign T1) and **+3** for season-long outright markets, which had been sitting at the same tier as the match markets. Migration `0106`. 1 231 of 1 870 tournaments carried no `risk_tier` — 98% of them Fonbet traditional-sport competitions, which no feed tiers. RiskZilla prices a NULL at tier 10, so none of that was over-exposed; it was under-traded and invisible, and there was no way to tell an unreviewed row from a feed-assigned one. ZAGI now reviews them: `risk_tier_source` gains a third value (`zagi`), the backoffice shows a green **ZAGI** chip with the model's one-line reasoning on hover, and a 30-minute sweeper picks up anything new so the backlog cannot rebuild itself. **Every assignment loosens the book**, so the model's verdict is clamped in code to a per-sport ceiling (`SPORT_TIER_CEILING`) and to T9 for simulations, an unparseable reply writes nothing, and the admin panel defaults to a **Preview** that shows the proposed tiers without applying them. Verified before shipping: 28 unit tests on the parser + clamp, 234 api tests, 11 workspaces typechecking, feed-ingester building/vetting/testing, the migration exercised against production's real schema in a rolled-back transaction, and a live 45-row sample judged correctly (World Cup T1, handball Worlds T3, FC 26 simulation T10, an injection probe planted in a tournament name ignored and rated T9). |
| Per-bettor promo visibility | Live (2026-05-19) | Migration `0071_bettor_promo_visibility` adds `bettor_promo_visibility_config` keyed by `(user_id, promo_kind ∈ {zillaflash, combi_boost}, scope, ref-id)` with one typed FK per scope tier and partial unique indexes per (scope, ref). Default `visible=true` (opt-out model) so a fresh user sees every promo; the operator toggles off per bettor via the admin UI. Cascade at read time: `match > tournament > sport > global` per (user, kind); first explicit row wins. Plumbed into [`/catalog/zillaflash`](./services/api/src/modules/zillaflash/routes.ts) (filters offers per-match) and [`/catalog/combi-boost-config`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts) (returns `enabled=false` when global combi_boost is hidden). Placement: [`/bets`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/routes.ts) rejects ZillaFlash legs the bettor can't see; [`bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts) silently drops combi-boost multiplier when ANY leg resolves to hidden — defense in depth even if the storefront slipped. Tiple / tippot / betbuilder are unaffected (those products don't use either promo). Admin: routes in [`services/api/src/modules/admin/promo-visibility.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/promo-visibility.ts) (every mutation audit-logged). UI at [`/admin/users/[id]/promo-visibility`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/promo-visibility/) — tree-style cascade editor with three `On / Off` toggle columns per row (ZillaFlash + CombiBoost + ZillaBuild) and a Clear button per kind to drop the override. User-detail picks up a summary card. |
| ZillaBuild (pre-built BetBuilder cards) | Live (2026-06-29) | Migration `0082_zillabuild` adds `zillabuild_config` (singleton) + `zillabuild_cards` and extends the `bettor_promo_kind` enum (migration 0071) with a third value `zillabuild`. Per prematch match where Oddin BetBuilder is available, the engine ([`services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/engine.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/engine.ts)) assembles up to `cards_per_map × map_count` cards (default 2 on Map 1 + 2 on Map 2), each a random 2-4 leg single-map OBB combo validated + priced via `sessionCreate`. A card's composition is chosen once and persisted (kept while its legs still price); odds are re-quoted from OBB on every open and never stored — so cards stay stable but prices stay fresh. `GET /catalog/matches/:id/zillabuild` ([routes.ts](./services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/routes.ts)) applies the per-bettor `zillabuild` promo-visibility gate (display-only — a card just pre-loads the standard BetBuilder slip, so `/bets` is unchanged). "Show what's buildable": a sparse map yields fewer cards; zero valid → the section hides. Admin config at `/admin/zillabuild-config` ([page](./apps/web/src/app/admin/zillabuild/)) controls the master on/off, the eligible `provider_market_id` allowlist (empty = all OBB-eligible per-map markets), card shape (cards-per-map / map-count / legs range), the min combined-odds floor, and the response cache TTL. Storefront widget ([`zillabuild-cards.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/match/zillabuild-cards.tsx)) renders the cards prematch-only between the ZillaFacts band and the markets tree; "Use this build" loads the combo into the slip's existing BetBuilder mode (standard `betType:"betbuilder"` placement). **Graceful-idle**: when `ODDIN_OBB_HOST` is empty or OBB is down the section is silently absent, same as the BetBuilder toggle. |
| Support AI assistant | Live, gated on `SUPPORT_AI_BOT_TOKEN` (2026-06-04; **moved into the docker stack 2026-09-01**) | Migration `0080_support_ai_bot` adds `support_messages.via_ai`, `support_threads.ai_handling` + `ai_paused_at`, and seeds an `is_ai` support user (`AI_SUPPORT_USER_ID`). Server: secret-auth, CSRF-exempt routes under `/webhooks/support-ai/:secret` ([`services/api/src/modules/support/bot/`](./services/api/src/modules/support/bot/) — `pending` work queue with read-only [account-facts](./services/api/src/modules/support/bot/account-facts.ts), `reply`, `escalate`, `heartbeat`), reachable publicly via Caddy's `/api/*` proxy. Bot replies attribute to the AI user (`sender_kind='admin'` + `via_ai`), bump unread, and publish the existing `SupportMessageFrame`. Admin: `/admin/support/threads/:id/take-over` + `resume-ai` + `ai-status`; a human reply auto-pauses the AI; the backoffice thread view gets an AI badge, a Take over / Resume AI button, and an Assistant-online dot. Worker: [`services/support-ai-bot`](./services/support-ai-bot/) **runs in the docker stack** (`mem_limit: 256m`, no ports — it dials OUT only and listens on nothing, so its healthcheck reads a liveness file the poll loop touches rather than probing a port). It was PC-only until 2026-09-01 for one reason — a local LM Studio — and pointing `LM_STUDIO_BASE_URL` at a hosted OpenAI-compatible endpoint removed it, so the assistant now answers around the clock instead of only while a PC is awake with a model loaded. Its sibling `zillaboost-banner-gen` stays on the PC permanently (ComfyUI). **Graceful-idle is load-bearing here**: with `SUPPORT_AI_BOT_TOKEN` unset the worker parks, warns and stays healthy instead of exiting, because `restart: unless-stopped` would otherwise crashloop it; `index.ts` checks the required env BEFORE dynamically importing `worker.ts`, which resolves config at module scope. Dials OUT only, talks to LM Studio (`/v1/chat/completions`, auto-discovers the loaded model). **Any OpenAI-compatible endpoint works, not just a local LM Studio** — point `LM_STUDIO_BASE_URL` at a hosted gateway (plus `LM_STUDIO_API_KEY`, which the client already sends as a bearer token) and the assistant stops depending on an operator PC being awake with a model loaded, which is the one thing keeping it off the Hetzner box. Two gotchas verified against `llm.oddin.gg` on 2026-09-01: the base URL takes **no** `/v1` suffix (the client appends it), and `LM_STUDIO_MODEL` must be pinned because auto-discovery takes the FIRST entry from `/v1/models`, which on a multi-model gateway is arbitrary. Reasoning models also spend `BOT_MAX_TOKENS` on an internal pass before emitting `content`, so don't lower it — starving the budget returns an empty reply with `finish_reason: "length"`, which the worker cannot answer from and hands off to a human with the holding message. That is exactly what happened on 2026-09-02 to "check my last bets, were they bad?": the old 1024 default covered small talk but the reasoning pass over ten tickets alone exceeded it (measured 1115 reasoning tokens on a smaller synthetic prompt). Defaults are now `BOT_MAX_TOKENS=50000` (a ceiling, the model stops on its own) and `BOT_LM_TIMEOUT_MS=120000`, both also pinned in the production `.env`; the worker logs a `completion` line per model turn with `finishReason` + prompt / completion / reasoning token counts, and an empty reply escalates with reason `empty_reply_truncated` when the cause was the budget. Tool calling and the tool-result round-trip both verified working on GLM-5.3-flash, and loops poll→reply/escalate with guardrails (responsible-gambling tripwire + payout/refund post-filter; uses only server-supplied account figures, can't move money). Read-only tools: `find_matches` / `match_markets` / `team_results` (Oddzilla data) plus `web_search` — a keyless Wikipedia lookup for real-world facts the platform lacks (past tournament winners, team / player / event background), with question-to-keyword query normalisation (`WIKIPEDIA_API_BASE` overrides the endpoint). Graceful-idle when `SUPPORT_AI_BOT_TOKEN` is unset. |
| Custom Boosted Odds (admin-curated) | Live (2026-08-26; selection scope 2026-08-27) | Migration `0085_boosted_odds`. Backoffice **Boosted Odds** section (below ZillaFlash) renders the full catalog tree — sports → tournaments + teams → matches → markets → selections — with a green Boost button on every row; the popup assigns boost % (Netwinstable key delta, ZillaFlash math), optional end time, optional Min Risk Score (bettors below it see standard prices). Storefront match page overlays the boosted price + green BOOST chip on covered outcomes (countdown only when an end time is set); boosted prices are computed client-side from the live WS outcome state via the shared `quoteMarketBoost`, so they move tick-for-tick with the feed (the 5 s poll carries only rule changes + the per-viewer RS gate); placement re-validates the rule server-side and pays at the boosted `odds_at_placement`; bet-delay skips per-leg drift for boosted legs. Promo banners shipped same day (migration 0086, `banner` flag per rule): home-page surfaces per scope (market → ZillaFlash-style card, match → scoreless match card whose odds column falls back match winner → current map winner → first active market, tournament → ZillaBoost banner, sport → sidebar bolt), popup gains duration presets (15m..24h) + Create promo banner checkbox, admin board is storefront-shaped with market multiselect bulk-boost and Edit on active-rules rows. Boost chip renders once per market card (never per cell); boosted buttons show the crossed-out pre-boost price. Banner prices carry the same selected affordance a match-row odd button does (accent fill, re-click removes) — without it a lobby pick filled the slip but left the banner looking untouched. A cell that has BOTH a boost and a ZillaTips badge passes `badgeOverlay` to `OddButton`, which reserves room on the label row: `boosted` right-aligns the label to sit over the active price, which is exactly where the tips chip is anchored, so the badge was covering the outcome label. **Selection scope** (migrations `0087`+`0088`): every market row in the admin board carries an expand caret listing the market's live selections with their current bettor-facing prices, each with its own Boost / Edit button. A selection boost takes the key delta out of that outcome's own implied probability so its siblings keep their raw price, and it REPLACES any coarser rule on that market (composing them would push the book past fair) — the market row shows a `N sel` chip and greys its own badge to `inactive` to say so. Clamped by the fair-book floor plus a per-outcome half-of-its-own-probability ceiling, with scarce headroom shared proportionally across co-boosted selections. On the storefront only the boosted cells wear the green styling; the market's header chip still renders once. No banner surface for a single selection. |
| Cookie notice banner + privacy page | Live (2026-07-02; reworked twice same day) | Banner is a **pure informational notice** — cookies, third-party embeds, and fe-analytics are on by default; the **two "Accept all" buttons** (operator's requested shape) acknowledge and dismiss it, gating nothing. Evolution: v1 offered real choices (Necessary only / Customize, honestly enforced), v2 cut the reject path (acceptance-only, still gating), v3 (current) removed the gates entirely per operator direction "all tracking on by default". Dismissal stored in localStorage `oz:cookie-consent` v2; older shapes re-show the notice once. `/privacy` states plainly that everything is active by default and points to browser-level cookie controls (**operator legal entity + registered address are still bracketed placeholders**). Footer, sidebar, and auth-layout links. If a reject control is ever reintroduced it must genuinely disable the categories. |
| FE analytics (first-party) | Live (2026-07-02), on by default | Migration `0083_analytics`. Self-hosted storefront analytics — no third-party vendor sees bettor traffic. Tracker in the web root layout captures page views, click order (per-session monotonic seq), sampled mouse trails and heartbeats; batches to `POST /analytics/collect` every 5 s with a sendBeacon tail flush. Admin dashboard at `/admin/analytics`: KPIs (sessions, signed-in share, unique users, views, clicks, avg/median session length), sessions/day, popular sections + pages, top clicked elements, and a per-session journey view with SVG mouse-trail replay. Retention: 90 d events/sessions, 14 d mouse trails (hourly sweep, Redis NX lock). Anonymous visitors tracked; session links to the user on first authed flush. **On by default** (operator direction 2026-07-02) — the cookie banner is an informational notice, not a gate; the banner body and `/privacy` disclose the tracking, so keep those in sync if this ever changes. Only exclusions: `navigator.webdriver` clients and `/admin` paths. |
| Bifrost backup feed (odds + settlement standby) | Live, gated on `BIFROST_API_KEY` (2026-09-03) | New Go service `services/bifrost-feed` + `oddin.backup` stream consumers in feed-ingester and settlement + Bifrost fixture fallback in the auto-mapper + manual risk tier (migration 0094). Oddin asked for a backup consuming their Bifrost GraphQL (the front end behind `maxbet.rs/en/esport`) and authorised MaxBet's brand key (client 101). Assessed 2026-09-03: covers fixtures, tournaments, markets, odds (identical to our feed on a prematch check), live scores, match lifecycle and settlement statuses (WON / LOST / HALF_* / VOIDED / INACTIVE); missing probabilities (derived from odds), risk tier (manual), and any cancel / rollback representation (deferred). Standby by default: publishes only after 45 s of AMQP silence, re-emits every cached snapshot on activation, stands down the instant AMQP resumes. Dry run against live Bifrost: 154 matches, 749 frames / 75 s, 312 odds_change + 2 bet_settlement, no errors. **Handicap sign fixed 2026-09-04**: Bifrost states a `handicap` from the opposite side to the AMQP feed (a -1.5 line priced shorter than the moneyline), so the parser flips it onto the AMQP convention — see the Where-things-live row for the evidence and why it belongs at key level rather than in the label. Runbook in docs/OPERATIONS.md, design in docs/BIFROST_BACKUP_FEED.md. |
| Prometheus + Grafana | Not started | Defer until traffic justifies |

## Local secrets that exist (DO NOT commit)

`D:\AI\Oddzilla\.env` (gitignored, local dev) and
`/home/team/oddzilla/.env` on the server (mode 600) hold:
- `ODDIN_TOKEN=<redacted>` (Sasha's Oddin integration token; in `.env`, never in docs)
- `ODDIN_CUSTOMER_ID=142` — fetched via `curl -H "x-access-token: $ODDIN_TOKEN" https://api-mq.integration.oddin.gg/v1/users/whoami` (note `/v1/` prefix; the legacy `/users/whoami` returns 404)
- `ODDIN_AMQP_PORT=5672` (NOT 5671 — Oddin runs AMQPS on 5672 per their docs §2)
- `ODDIN_AMQP_TLS=true`
- `FONBET_ENABLED` — env DEFAULT for the Fonbet sports feed (no credentials; the line is public; default `false` idles the container on `/healthz`). Since migration 0099 the runtime position is the **Fonbet feed** switch on `/admin/feed` (`feed_control.fonbet_enabled`, `PUT /admin/feed/fonbet`): once an operator has set it, the Postgres value wins over this env var and survives restarts and deploys; the env var only applies while the column is NULL. Turning the switch OFF suspends every Fonbet market and stops polling within 2 s; ON boots the feed in place, no container restart. **`FONBET_SETTLE_ENABLED` is a SEPARATE switch, default `false`** — the results-based grader pays real wallets and must be soaked against Fonbet's own results on staging before it is enabled (docs/FONBET.md "Before enabling settlement"); with the feed on and settlement off, Fonbet markets stay open for manual settlement. **Site + language:** `FONBET_SITE_ORIGIN=https://fon.bet`, `FONBET_URLS_JSON`, `FONBET_LINE_HOSTS`, `FONBET_COMMON_HOSTS`, `FONBET_LOGO_CDN` (read by the api too, for the sidebar's category flags), `FONBET_SCOPE_MARKET=1600` and `FONBET_LANG=en` are a matched set — `scopeMarket` is per site (fon.bet 1600 / fonbet.kz 1800, each 404s on the other) and the host lists are the trust anchor for `urls.json` discovery, so moving only some of them rejects every discovered host. `.env.example` carries the complete Kazakhstan set as a comment. `FONBET_LANG` is en|ru ONLY — the settlement grader parses Fonbet's own text and carries just those two vocabularies. The other `FONBET_*` tuning knobs and `FONBET_INGESTER_HEALTH_PORT=8087` have defaults in `.env.example`. fonbet-ingester also reads `ODDS_PUBLISHER_GROUP` (same default as odds-publisher) to pace its cold-start republish on that group's lag.
- `SETTLEMENT_EXTERNAL_STREAM=settlement.external` — Redis stream services/settlement consumes for provider-neutral settlements (Fonbet). Empty disables the consumer, and Fonbet tickets then never settle.
- `ODDS_HISTORY_SKIP_PMID_MIN=0` — odds-publisher brake on `odds_history` growth: ticks with `provider_market_id >=` this value skip the history INSERT (`published_odds` still updates). `0` keeps history for every tick; `1000000` (the Fonbet namespace) drops it for the Fonbet line if its tick volume outruns the partition retention — see docs/OPERATIONS.md "odds_history retention".
- `JWT_SECRET` + `REFRESH_COOKIE_SECRET` — generated 48-byte secrets
- `POSTGRES_PASSWORD` — generated 64-char hex
- `NODE_ENV=production` (required so auth cookies get `Secure` flag)
- `FRONTEND_HOST=oddzilla.cc`, `ADMIN_HOST=sadmin.oddzilla.cc`, `ACME_EMAIL=alexander.chernavin@oddin.gg` — consumed by Caddy for per-host reverse-proxy + Let's Encrypt. The Caddyfile also serves a hard-coded 301 from the legacy `s.oddzilla.cc` to the apex.
- `COOKIE_DOMAIN=.oddzilla.cc` — **legacy**; auth cookies are now host-scoped (set without a Domain attribute), and this value is consumed only by the legacy-cookie-kill migration helper in [`services/api/src/lib/cookies.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/cookies.ts) and by the locale-preference cookie in [`apps/web/src/lib/i18n/actions.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/i18n/actions.ts) (where shared scope still makes sense — language follows the user across subdomains). Safe to drop ~30 days after the 2026-05-18 host-scope cutover once every long-lived refresh cookie has rotated through the new path.
- `CORS_ORIGINS=https://oddzilla.cc,https://sadmin.oddzilla.cc`
- `CORS_ORIGINS_STRICT` — ws-gateway only; **not set in the production `.env`** and does not need to be: `docker-compose.yml` defaults it to `true` (2026-09-03), which refuses a WebSocket upgrade that carries no Origin header at all. Browsers always send one, so this only turns away bare scripts. Set `CORS_ORIGINS_STRICT=false` in `.env` if a legitimate Origin-less client ever needs `/ws`.
- `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL=` — **both empty on prod**. Browser falls back to `/api` and `wss://<host>/ws` via Caddy. Non-empty values bake `http://localhost:3001` into the prod bundle and break in-browser auth. For local `pnpm dev`, put non-empty values in `apps/web/.env.local`.
- `HD_MASTER_MNEMONIC=` **empty** — withdrawal/deposit features inert until set. Generate any BIP39 phrase.
- `TRON_RPC_URL` defaults to `https://api.trongrid.io`
- `ETH_RPC_URL=` **empty** — ERC20 scanner inert until provided
- `BACKUP_GPG_RECIPIENT=` (optional) — set to an off-host operator's GPG key id and the daily pg dump is encrypted in addition to gzipped (`.sql.gz.gpg`). Leave unset to keep the gzip-only behaviour.
- `DISIR_BRAND_TOKEN=` Oddin Disir widget brand token. Held only by the api process; the frontend never sees it. When empty the `/widgets/*` routes 503 `widget_disabled` and the storefront silently skips rendering the iframes. Per-environment — request a separate token for `integration` vs `main`.
- `DISIR_BASE_URL=https://api-disir.oddin.gg` (optional override for staging endpoints).
- `DISIR_ENV=integration|main` — written into the upstream URL path (`/statistics/{env}/match/...`). Stays at `integration` until we cut over the entire stack to Oddin production. **Note:** every domain hosting a widget iframe must be whitelisted by Oddin support — currently `oddzilla.cc` (post-migration; the old `s.oddzilla.cc` is still on the allowlist while it 301s) plus any dev hosts you add via `pnpm dev`. Email `support@oddin.gg` with the host list when adding a new environment.
- `ODDIN_OBB_HOST=` Oddin BetBuilder gRPC endpoint (e.g. `api-obb.integration.oddin.gg:443` for integration, `api-obb.oddin.gg:443` for prod). Empty = `/betbuilder/*` 503s and the storefront toggle hides. **IP allowlist required**: each calling environment must be whitelisted on Oddin's side before flipping this on. `ODDIN_OBB_TLS=true` (default) for both integration and prod. Token resolves from `ODDIN_OBB_TOKEN` first, then `ODDIN_TOKEN` — Oddin currently issues a single per-customer token usable for both feed + OBB.
- `ODDIN_VIDEO_BASE_URL=https://feed-dev.oddin-video.gg` — Oddin video (Havik) feed API base. Integration is `feed-dev`, production `https://feed.oddin-video.gg`. Every other host the SDK uses is derived from it by hostname convention (`playback[-dev]`, `drm[-dev]`, `events.feed[-dev]`, `beacons[-dev]`), so this one value switches the whole environment. Oddin also publishes a directory at `https://status-dev.oddin-video.gg/v1/endpoints.json` if we ever want discovery instead of config.
- `ODDIN_VIDEO_API_KEY=` — Oddin video publishable api-key (`pk_test_…` integration / `pk_live_…` production). Empty = `/video/*` 503s `video_disabled` and the storefront renders no player and no stream tab. **This one is deliberately not a secret**: Oddin's model is a browser-safe key scoped by a server-side allowed-origin list, and the SDK needs it in the page to sign DRM licence requests. It is still delivered from the api at runtime (never `NEXT_PUBLIC_*`) so rotating it doesn't require rebuilding the storefront bundle. Every origin that mounts the player must be on Oddin's allowed-origin list — email the host list to Oddin, same as the Disir widget domains.
- `METRICS_COLLECTOR_URL=` (optional) override for the metrics-collector base URL the api proxies for `/admin/monitoring/*`. Default `http://metrics-collector:9090` resolves the compose service name on the docker default network — leave it unset in production. When the collector is down or the URL is wrong the api returns 503 `monitoring_unavailable` and the admin page renders "metrics unavailable" without crashing.
- `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_PATH=/run/firebase/service-account.json` — path INSIDE the api container to the Firebase Admin SDK service-account JSON. The compose mount maps `${FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_HOST:-/srv/oddzilla-firebase}` (host) → `/run/firebase` (container, read-only). When the JSON file isn't there the push-outbox worker logs `firebase: disabled` and stamps every pending row with `last_error='firebase_disabled'` so the queue drains; flipping the env on for real means dropping the JSON at `FIREBASE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_HOST/service-account.json` (mode 600, owned by uid 1000 = the api container's node user) and `make recreate api`. Generation procedure in [`apps/mobile-android/.../fcm/README.md`](./apps/mobile-android/app/src/main/java/cc/oddzilla/app/fcm/README.md).
- `EMAIL_PROVIDER=resend|sendgrid|ses|smtp` (default `resend`) — selects the email client implementation. Only `resend` ships today; the other values are placeholders for a one-file addition.
- `EMAIL_PROVIDER_TOKEN=` — Resend API key (or the equivalent for whichever provider you select). Empty = the email-outbox worker still runs but drains rows with `last_error='email_disabled'` so the queue stays bounded. Resend free tier is 3 000 emails / month (well above MVP scale); generate the key at https://resend.com/api-keys after verifying the send domain.
- `EMAIL_PROVIDER_SECRET=` — second credential. Used by `ses` (AWS secret key) and ignored by the rest.
- `EMAIL_FROM=noreply@oddzilla.cc` — must be a verified sender on the provider.
- `EMAIL_REPLY_TO=` (optional) — defaults to `EMAIL_FROM` when empty.
- `EMAIL_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=` (optional) — base URL the worker embeds in verify / reset links. Empty falls back to `https://${FRONTEND_HOST}`; set explicitly when a reverse-proxy or locale-prefix means the simple derivation isn't right.
- `SENDGRID_INBOUND_SECRET=` — secret path component for the inbound-mail webhook (`/webhooks/sendgrid-inbound/<secret>`). Used by BOTH the api (constant-time-compared at the route, 404 on miss) AND the [`services/mail-receiver`](./services/mail-receiver/) container (paths the secret into its POSTs to the api over the docker network). Required for mail-receiver to boot — empty value fails-fast with a clear error. Generate with `openssl rand -hex 24`. The route slug retains `sendgrid-inbound` from before we moved off SendGrid; the multipart shape IS the contract, so when we eventually swap inbound providers the api code doesn't need to change.
- `SUPPORT_AI_BOT_TOKEN=` (optional) — auth for the Gemma support assistant's webhook routes (`/webhooks/support-ai/<secret>`, migration 0080). The PC-side worker [`services/support-ai-bot`](./services/support-ai-bot/) puts it in the URL path (constant-time compared, 404 on miss). Empty = those routes 503 `bot_disabled` and the admin Assistant-online dot stays offline (chat falls back to humans). The SAME value goes in the worker's own `.env`. Generate with `openssl rand -hex 24`.
- `BANNER_GEN_TOKEN=` (optional) — auth for the ZillaBoost graphics-banner worker's webhook routes (`/webhooks/banner-gen/<secret>`, migration 0089). The PC-side worker [`services/zillaboost-banner-gen`](./services/zillaboost-banner-gen/) puts it in the URL path (constant-time compared, 404 on miss). Empty = those routes 503 `banner_gen_disabled`; the admin "Generate graphics banner" option still enqueues jobs, which wait in `zillaboost_banner_image_jobs` until the worker can reach them. The SAME value goes in the worker's own `.env`. Generate with `openssl rand -hex 24`.

- `SPORTRADAR_SYNC_DISABLED=` / `SPORTRADAR_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES=30` — the background Sportradar mapping sweep (migration 0100). **No credential involved**: Sportradar's statistics host answers ordinary server-to-server requests, unlike their LMT host, which is licensed per embedding origin and 403s everything else. `SPORTRADAR_SYNC_DISABLED=1` stops the sweep; the admin Sync button still works. Interval floor is 5 minutes, default 30 — comfortably inside the 8-day pairing window.
- `SPORTRADAR_LLM_BASE_URL=https://llm.oddin.gg/v1` / `SPORTRADAR_LLM_API_KEY=` / `SPORTRADAR_LLM_MODEL=glm-5.3-flash` — the model that adjudicates the Sportradar mapping queue. **Unlike the support-ai bot's `LM_STUDIO_BASE_URL`, this one INCLUDES the `/v1`** — the adjudicator posts to `<base>/chat/completions` directly rather than through a client that appends it. Pin the model: auto-discovery on a multi-model gateway takes an arbitrary first entry. Empty API key = no adjudication and the queue stays human-reviewed (the sweep and the storefront are unaffected). `max_tokens` is 16 000 per batch of 25 because this model spends roughly two thirds of its budget on an internal reasoning pass before emitting any text — a tight cap returns an empty reply and adjudicates nothing, the same trap documented for the support bot.
- `ZAGI_BASE_URL=https://llm.oddin.gg/v1` / `ZAGI_API_KEY=` / `ZAGI_MODEL=glm-5.3-flash` — **ZillaAGI**, the in-house LLM, and the canonical credentials for every model-assisted feature in the api. Same gateway and same three gotchas as the Sportradar trio above (`/v1` included in the base, model pinned, generous `max_tokens` because the reasoning pass eats the budget first); [`services/api/src/lib/zagi/client.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/zagi/client.ts) falls back to `SPORTRADAR_LLM_*` when these are unset, so a deployment that already configured that gateway gets ZAGI without an `.env` edit. Empty API key = every ZAGI feature idles.
- `ZAGI_RISK_TIER_DISABLED=` / `ZAGI_RISK_TIER_INTERVAL_MINUTES=30` — the tournament risk-tier review sweep (migration 0106). `=1` stops the sweep; the `/admin/tournaments` Run button still works. Interval floor 5 minutes, 200 rows per pass.
- `TOURNAMENT_LOGO_SWEEPER_DISABLED=` / `TOURNAMENT_LOGO_INTERVAL_MINUTES=60` — the tournament logo sweep (migration 0108), which fills the marks neither feed carries from Liquipedia (esports) and Wikidata (everything else). Uses the `ZAGI_*` credentials; idle without them. Interval floor 10 minutes, and each sweep runs to a 10-minute budget rather than a row count because Liquipedia asks for 2 s between API calls. No credential of its own — both sources are public, and the client sends an identifying User-Agent with contact details, which is what their etiquette asks for.
- `BIFROST_API_KEY=` (optional) — brand key for Oddin's Bifrost GraphQL API, read by BOTH [`services/bifrost-feed`](./services/bifrost-feed/) (the backup odds + settlement feed) and feed-ingester (fixture fallback for the auto-mapper). Oddin authorised Oddzilla to use MaxBet's key (Bifrost client 101, "MaxBet RS") on 2026-09-03; the value is in the page source of `maxbet.rs/en/esport` but lives only in `.env` here. Empty = bifrost-feed idles and the auto-mapper has no fallback. Companions: `BIFROST_API_URL` (default `https://api-bifrost.oddin.gg/main/bifrost/query`; the wss URL derives from it), `BIFROST_LOCALE=en`, `BIFROST_ORIGIN=https://bifrost.oddin.gg` (must be a host the key is registered for), `BIFROST_MODE=auto|active|off`, `BIFROST_TAKEOVER_AFTER_SECONDS=45` (keep it above `FEED_STALE_SUSPEND_SECONDS`), `BIFROST_RESYNC_INTERVAL_SECONDS=300`, `BACKUP_STREAM_ENABLED=true` (feed-ingester + settlement consume `oddin.backup`; `false` detaches), `BIFROST_FEED_HEALTH_PORT=8086`.

`.gitignore` excludes `.env` and `.claude/`. The local repo IS now
git-initialized and pushed to https://github.com/alexanderchernavin-cloud/oddzilla1
(private). The server keeps an authoritative `.env` at
`/home/team/oddzilla/.env` with mode 600. **Never read .env over SSH** —
the sandbox blocks it (would dump secrets into the transcript). Patch
specific keys with `sed -i 's/^KEY=.*/KEY=value/'` instead.

## Verification commands

When in doubt that everything is wired up, run from repo root:

```bash
pnpm -r typecheck    # 9 TS workspaces (web, api, ws-gateway, 6 packages)
pnpm audit --prod    # should report 0 vulns
for svc in feed-ingester fonbet-ingester odds-publisher settlement bifrost-feed bet-delay wallet-watcher; do
  (cd services/$svc && go vet ./... && go test ./...)
done
```

All should be silent / green. Last known good state: end of the security
audit pass (PR #130, 2026-05-06) — see [`docs/PHASES.md`](./docs/PHASES.md)
for the commit-by-commit changelog.

**Audit-log integrity probe** — to confirm no row in `admin_audit_log`
has been tampered with after insert:

```sh
ssh team@178.104.174.24 'set -a; . /home/team/oddzilla/.env; set +a; sudo -n docker exec oddzilla-postgres-1 psql -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$POSTGRES_DB" -c "SELECT COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE ok) AS valid, COUNT(*) FILTER (WHERE NOT ok) AS broken, COUNT(*) AS total FROM admin_audit_chain_check();"'
```

`broken` should always be `0`. Any non-zero value is the canonical
"someone edited the audit log via direct DB access" signal.

For the production server, after a `git push` to main:

```bash
ssh team@178.104.174.24 "cd /home/team/oddzilla && make deploy"
```

That's it. The `make deploy` target ([`infra/deploy/deploy.sh`](./infra/deploy/deploy.sh))
does fetch → diff → pre-deploy `pg_dump` (if any migration is pending)
→ `pnpm db:migrate` → parallel-build the changed services → SHA-tag
each built image → `--no-deps --force-recreate` non-web → rolling
recreate web1→web2→web3 → smoke test the storefront + API.

Companion targets:

```bash
make deploy-status   # dry-run: show what 'make deploy' would deploy
make rollback        # docker tag previous-sha → :latest + recreate
```

Rollback is image-only — DB migrations are not auto-reverted. Our
convention is forward-only nullable-additive, so the previous code
version reads a newer schema cleanly. If a migration was destructive,
restore from `.deploy/backups/<sha>.sql.gz` manually before rollback.

State the deploy keeps under `/home/team/oddzilla/.deploy/`:

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `last-sha` | Atomically-written marker of the last successful deploy. `deploy-status` diffs from here. |
| `log` | Append-only newline log of every `deploy` / `rollback` / `smoke_fail` event with timestamp, SHA, and services touched. `tail -f` during incidents. |
| `images/<svc>` | Per-service stack of the last 3 SHAs ever built. Source of truth for rollback. |
| `backups/<sha>.sql.gz` | Pre-deploy `pg_dump` taken right before a migration runs. Only the most recent is retained (`PRE_DEPLOY_BACKUP_RETENTION`, default 1 since 2026-07-02) — dumps have grown to ~8.3 GB each, and keeping 2 plus one in-flight nearly filled the 150 GB box mid-deploy. `/var/backups/oddzilla/` (14-day cron) is the durable history line. |

Concurrency: `flock` on `/var/lock/oddzilla-deploy.lock` — two
operators running `make deploy` at the same time get a clean "another
deploy is in progress" error instead of a race.

The serial-build constraint that used to live in this section is
gone. CPX31 (8 GB) handles parallel builds; if a future regression
OOMs again, set `DEPLOY_BUILD_PARALLEL_CAP=2` in the deploy shell
(maps to `COMPOSE_PARALLEL_LIMIT`) and re-run.

> Migration → build → recreate is the right order. The new column
> appears before the new code reads it; running images can ignore
> a nullable column they don't know about. The deploy script enforces
> this order — don't run `pnpm db:migrate` or `docker compose build`
> by hand on the box unless you have a reason that doesn't fit
> `make deploy`.
>
> `--no-deps` keeps postgres / redis / caddy untouched across each
> service recreate. Bouncing them would needlessly drop active WS
> connections + reset the postgres connection pool.
>
> SSH+`sg docker` is a trap (`$`-escaping, see the
> `project_ssh_dollar_escaping` memory) — `sudo -n docker compose`
> is the safe form. Every script under `infra/deploy/` uses it.

## Handoff notes for the next session

If you're a fresh agent picking this up:

1. **Read this file first** (you're here). Then skim
   [`docs/PHASES.md`](./docs/PHASES.md) for what's done and what's next.
2. **All phases 1–8, Phase 10 (community), the post-Phase-8 hardening,
   AND the security audit pass (PR #130) are live and deployed.**
   The news scraper was cancelled mid-Phase-8 (migration 0003 dropped
   the table; `services/news-scraper/` is gone). The next layer of work
   is the **pre-launch exit gates** — KYC, signer isolation, daily
   wallet reconciliation, off-host encrypted backups (the script now
   supports `BACKUP_GPG_RECIPIENT`; the rsync target is still manual),
   runbook, monitoring. These are blockers for accepting real user
   traffic, not for further dev work.
3. **Production is running.** GitHub remote is
   https://github.com/alexanderchernavin-cloud/oddzilla1 (private).
   Server is `team@178.104.174.24` with the full Docker Compose stack
   live. Connected to Oddin's integration broker (bookmaker 142) via
   AMQPS on 5672. See "Verification commands" above for the redeploy
   recipe.
4. **Before touching anything money-related**, re-read invariants 3 + 4
   above. The two-layer apply-once (settlements unique 5-tuple + ledger
   unique partial index, with the new `<ticketID>:N` generation suffix
   for re-settlements) is what keeps the books consistent under crash,
   replay, and race conditions.
5. **Don't trust phase comments inside source files** — phase progress
   moved fast. CLAUDE.md's "Live phase status" table is the truth.
6. **When debugging the Oddin feed, always test from inside a container
   on the compose network** (`docker compose exec feed-ingester sh -c
   "..."`) and from the host. Diagnostic asymmetry between the two
   (e.g. one can resolve `mq.integration.oddin.gg` and the other can't)
   has been a recurring source of confusion.
7. **Auto mode caveat.** The sandbox blocks reading the production
   `.env` over SSH (rightly — would dump secrets to transcript). Patch
   single keys with `sed`; don't `cat .env`.
8. **Browser auth debugging.** If login/signup fails with "Could not
   reach the server", first suspect `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` baked into
   the web bundle. Verify with
   `curl -s https://oddzilla.cc/_next/static/chunks/... | grep localhost:3001`
   — zero hits means the bundle is clean. The fix is to keep empty
   defaults in [`apps/web/next.config.ts`](./apps/web/next.config.ts).
9. **Design system.** Tokens live in
   [`apps/web/src/app/globals.css`](./apps/web/src/app/globals.css);
   primitives in
   [`apps/web/src/components/ui/primitives.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/ui/primitives.tsx).
   The shell (`top-bar`, `sidebar`, `bet-slip-rail`) wraps everything
   under `app/(main)/`. Sports order in `sidebar.tsx`: slugs in the `TOP`
   array are pinned to the top; everything else is alphabetical; slugs
   in `HIDDEN` never render (currently `efootballbots` +
   `ebasketballbots`, matching the feed-ingester `BLOCKED_ODDIN_SPORT_SLUGS`
   default). Admin / wallet / bets still use legacy Tailwind classes
   against the `@theme`-bridged color tokens; reskin them when the
   design gets extended.
10. **Odds margin.** Globally **0 bp** today — Oddin already margins.
    If the user asks to add house margin, do it via
    `/admin/margins` (cascade market_type→tournament→sport→global)
    rather than touching code. Per-user margin would be a new column
    (`users.margin_bp_override`) + a branch in
    `odds-publisher/internal/publisher/publisher.go` `Handle` (batched
    since the Fonbet feed landed: one UNNEST UPDATE + one odds_history
    INSERT per XREADGROUP batch, `store.ResolveMarkets` for the lineage).
